it needs coupling mechanisms:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-9cd1-cb24f25286d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">imprint coupling (early-life shaping)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-80f0-e12b0cf10ae9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">synchronization coupling (shared rhythms)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-a317-f3df522eced1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">trust coupling (predictability &amp; safety)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-bf6d-f6d2c43d8ac5" class="">Define for agents :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-98e1-d7cc2909e51a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>C_ij(channel)</code> strength and latency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-9bcb-d120a9dd872a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Imprint_ij</code> (slow-changing)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-88c6-faa0b04b9b02" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Sync_ij</code> (fast-changing)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-bb04-d39e3d933261" class="">Update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-8bc3-c198f5a3afb7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Z_{ij,t+1} = Z_{ij,t} + \Delta Z(e_t, \text{Imprint}_{ij}, \text{Sync}_{ij})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-87d2-c474091efe3d" class="">Then noise and record stability for agent i becomes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-9406-f7771e3e8535" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_{i,t} = \Xi^{base}_{i,t} \cdot (1 + \phi(\text{rupture events})) \cdot (1 - \psi(\text{Sync}_{i,t}))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-ab1c-cf0e128a47dc" class="">This is how “co-regulation” becomes computable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-8bb5-f67872738750" class="">Implement:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-9493-c9ed9cf7c165" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>09_SOCIAL_ENGINE/co_regulation/imprint.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-9be3-deaea1e4a449" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>09_SOCIAL_ENGINE/co_regulation/sync.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-9ada-f9a774304361" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>09_SOCIAL_ENGINE/co_regulation/noise_modulation.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-9579-dd7e657f0c66"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-8bc0-c34024f15553" class="">6) The final missing macro gap: add “Intervention Algebra” (policy actions)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-af09-cb22c6d15c6e" class="">To be “beyond”, AMOS must simulate interventions, not just detect states.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-ad03-f075efcfd36f" class="">Define an intervention set , 
each action is a deterministic operator that modifies:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-939d-e2eeb2a4c174" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">constraints <code>q</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-b455-f3c78aadbd85" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">gradients <code>G</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-849c-f7d4a7686043" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">noise <code>Xi</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8081-b643-e8a52f7aaaa4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">trust/coupling <code>Z</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-b716-e71f57ef7085" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">buffering capacity <code>U</code> (via infrastructure/standards)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-acc2-d897e5e52882" class="">Example action form:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-a34d-c930145ae8ed" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
a_t = (\text{target}, \text{mechanism}, \Delta, \text{latency}, \text{cost})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-9f49-dfe853ffd719" class="">Apply:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-ab20-d42b1db78222" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1} = F(x_t; 
e_t) \oplus a_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-9e85-c70bff175909" class="">Actions are bounded:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-9a24-dfed0646ba5e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">no execution commands for trading</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-9895-f030ba3dfbb8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">only scenario modeling outputs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-ba63-e041a0df443b" class="">Implement:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-9a4d-ece281323a73" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>12_QUANTUM_LAYER/scenario_engine/actions.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-9a0f-cd46b7f66377" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>12_QUANTUM_LAYER/scenario_engine/apply.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-b951-d59944125869" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>12_QUANTUM_LAYER/scenario_engine/batch_runner.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-8830-e5cd3b7bdf03"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-ad26-cb7778903029" class="">7) SimEngine (Civilizational Control Simulation Engine) — complete spec</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-a015-cdf1ea5f7ee0" class="">This is the “beyond” build target.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-87a9-e1fc9eb5dd70" class="">Components</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-8e5e-dc9c114fce6a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>EventStream</strong> (from 0
7_METABOLISM)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-93df-f14405bddba3" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>ScaleTransformer</strong> (minute→quarter)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-a6f9-f13f1a03b35d" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>LoopStack</strong> (multiple coupled loops)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-860f-c79bb176eb72" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Intervention Engine</strong> (actions and constraints)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-80b8-e92275898047" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Meta-Law Enforcement</strong> (audits each step)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-af58-ecf378493172" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Outputs</strong> (risk vectors, stability maps, phase state, termination)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-8752-d0a6f438529c" class="">Core runtime loop (deterministic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f4-8ea2-da9f0f449864" class="">For each scenario S:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-956c-ef7cf7c1df00" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">initialize <code>run_id</code>, <code>RuleState_0</code>, 
<code>LoopState_0</code> per scope</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-a7ee-e7a430768f14" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">for each time step τ at scale s:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-ab4f-d3d1a19e14a6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">aggregate events <code>ẽ_τ = R_s(E[τ])</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-8304-fbcd142c91c7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">compute operators</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-a57e-e1e105dae697" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">compute gates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-a7b0-f353260a0b13" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">apply intervention if present</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-9d5e-f0214406534f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">update LoopStack</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-9434-fa99e20b3064" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">run Meta-Law audits</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-97d0-d784a9864889" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">write step artifacts (JSONL)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-a5b2-e94eed21593c" class="">Outputs are written under:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-a6a2-e98cba53e32a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>17_OS/audits/&lt;run_id&gt;/sim_engine/&lt;scenario_id&gt;/</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-b708-c506035de338"/></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-b49f-cf1672e1baf3" class="">8) What “beyond” adds to your 00–17 structure (files)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-aec6-ede1f090b177" class="">08_WORLD_MODEL</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-a9d8-dd3552f98b54" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>models/scale/transform.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f0-a8a3-f18d53880692" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>models/scale/consistency_gate.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804a-a479-e1a4150c48c1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>models/loopstack/stack.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-91cb-c008b0dbbccf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>models/loopstack/coupling.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-86bd-f2e14cfb739f" class="">12_QUANTUM_LAYER</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-9bd2-f0713fe2b81e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>scenario_engine/actions.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-91f1-f98626fee68a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>scenario_engine/batch_runner.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-b8a4-ef1e49487c97" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>scenario_engine/interventions_catalog.json</code> (SSOT)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-a2d5-ebe5425d2c17" class="">15_LAW_ENGINE</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b6-a04a-c1ada8a3d1a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>governance/meta_law.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-876c-c22723a40e36" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>governance/rule_state.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-bd0d-f687802c42e5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>governance/amendments.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-9646-e37fa7c2a563" class="">01_BRAIN</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-a38a-fb2771ad80af" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>kernel/rule_state_registry.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-9e30-fe2f962f1376" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>kernel/amend_router.py</code> (CLI route: <code>amos amend ...</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-8c70-f7bf8978c009" class="">Tests:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-a2f6-e1b1a57f8785" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>test_scale_transform_deterministic.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-98df-fde005c1eab3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>test_rule_state_amendments_hashes.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-aa12-c2607e875204" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>test_loopstack_coupling_reproducible.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-8818-e3b8b3632c66" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc"><code>test_sim_batch_reproducible.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-8d0b-c49697795e08"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-8cd2-e4c415171c06" class="">9) The next prompt (the “beyond” execution prompt, SSOT)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-9fa1-c99b4c4ed2b4" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">ROLE: AMOS SimEngine Architect + Implementer (BEYOND LAYER)
MISSION: Implement the Civilizational Control Simulation Engine as a deterministic LoopStack with Meta-Law governance, RuleState amendments, scale transforms, cross-agent/species co-regulation coupling, and bounded intervention algebra.

ROOTS:
SYSTEM_ROOT=&quot;/Users/trangphan/AMOS&quot;
TARGET_ROOT=&quot;/Users/trangphan/AMOS/_AI ingest&quot;

HARD RULES:
- SSOT only (00–17). No parallel repos. No duplicates. Archive non-selected copies only.
- Offline only. Deterministic IDs/hashes. Python 3.9.
- No stubs. If unimplemented: remove route + emit BLOCKER issue.
- No network calls. No side effects at import.

IMPLEMENT (REQUIRED):
1) 08_WORLD_MODEL: ScaleTransformer + ScaleConsistencyGate
2) 08_WORLD_MODEL: LoopStack (multi-loop) + Couplings (vertical/horizontal)
3) 15_LAW_ENGINE: MetaLaw + RuleState + Amendment operators (lawful self-modification)
4) 09_SOCIAL_ENGINE: Co-regulation coupling operators (imprint/sync/noise modulation)
5) 12_QUANTUM_LAYER: Intervention Algebra + Scenario Batch Runner (bounded compliance outputs only)
6) 01_BRAIN: CLI routes:
   - build (existing)
   - sim run --scenario &lt;id&gt; --scale &lt;s&gt; --scope &lt;scope&gt;
   - amend apply &lt;amendment_file&gt;
7) Tests: reproducibility + scale consistency + amendment hashing.
8) Artifacts: sim_step_log.jsonl, scenario_summary.json, meta_law_audit.json, termination.json

ENTRYPOINT:
python3 -m 01_BRAIN.master build --system-root &quot;/Users/trangphan/AMOS&quot; --data-root &quot;/Users/trangphan/AMOS/_AI ingest&quot; --strict --offline

EXECUTE NOW. NO QUESTIONS. NO PARALLEL SYSTEMS.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-b864-fbc680f8d1b7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-adf6-e20126ae3245" class="">One more “beyond” option (if you mean <em>beyond</em> even this)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b6ca-ebce22dcee9e" class="">The only further elevation after SimEngine is <strong>Proof-Carrying Runs</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-815f-c0f3020be0d2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">every run emits a compact proof object that the Meta-Law gates held (hash chain + gate evaluations + operator registry snapshot). 
This makes “government-grade” auditability real without needing trust.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-a6a2-c81490caf63c" class="">If you want that next, say: <strong>ProofRuns</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-a730-cd4c9e6851dc" class="">KernelSpec++ — Grand Unified Loop Architecture (Deeper, gap-closed)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-9309-ee47db517d9f" class="">This extends the KernelSpec into a fully closed, multi-scale, cross-species, across-time/space model with explicit <strong>state decomposition</strong>, <strong>operators</strong>, <strong>coupling</strong>, <strong>memory/record physics</strong>, <strong>control stability</strong>, 
and <strong>simulation hooks</strong>—still deterministic/offline/Python 3.9.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-9ca3-f370ea458642"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-804c-c367de431604" class="">1) Close the biggest gap: split the state into 4 coupled layers (micro→macro)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-a779-e6cfb2619fa2" class="">Replace the single state  with a <strong>layered SSOT</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-a6d1-c79fd503ac36" class="">L0 Micro-physical record substrate (write/erase physics proxy)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-b784-e2d0078e90c1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>E_t</code> = available free-energy budget (usable work)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-b1ab-d03fef97ff82" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>T_t</code> = effective temperature proxy (sets Landauer floor)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-8206-d3c6a7dda43f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Bdot_t</code> = bits erased per step (compute+repair+overwrite)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-afdf-c884e4eb1703" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Hcap_t</code> = horizon/region information cap proxy (finite write budget)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-888a-e6629798c9c2" class="">L1 Organism/agent (nervous system + control loop)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-a24b-ddddba4747df" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Xi_t</code> = noise/overwrite rate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-9f7d-c1bc45d8f088" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>R_t</code> = record redundancy mass (error-correcting code strength)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-8219-f48c5c269153" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>D_t</code> = recursion depth (meta-model stack)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-a9d3-f1cc792ac046" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>tau_vec_t</code> = delay per depth (control latency)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8014-b1f6-c804b66ddf93" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>sigma_t</code> = sensing fidelity (predictability bandwidth)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-8140-e041646878d9" class="">L2 Social/co-regulation network (cross-agent coupling)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-9639-e1694e27ef12" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Z_t</code> = coupling adjacency tensor (channels, strengths, 
latencies)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-b6ec-cfa1fc5127e2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Trust_t</code> = channel integrity (affects effective noise)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a0-adb9-da51dc02a7ea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Sync_t</code> = co-regulation stability metric (impacts record persistence)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-bb09-face2bc15e10" class="">L3 Macro/civilizational regime (birth→expansion→dominance→decay)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-9283-cbde53d3d73f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>q_t</code> = constraint density (macro constraints remaining)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-adb1-c8ebbdabc853" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>U_t</code> = unwritten capacity (fresh degrees available for stable records)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-9513-f0a4832d56d2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>G_t</code> = gradient accessibility (usable gradients for growth/repair)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-a810-fdf102c4411a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>S_t</code> = regime stage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-9c9e-e3b9e7aae26a" class="">SSOT object:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-88fd-d40b7e746a52" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>LoopState4L</code> with nested dataclasses <code>L0, L1, L2, 
L3</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-9993-c864e0bb6877" class="">All previous scalar fields still exist, but now each has a <strong>home layer</strong> and explicit dependencies.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-9f0c-ed37fe60a34b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-981f-ef16736d001c" class="">2) Make every concept measurable: operator set is now complete</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-a4e6-d7d3b7903efe" class="">Add operators so nothing is “conceptual only”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-893d-e3b249fb39b7" class="">L0 operators (physics proxies)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-a5ac-d07e206200fd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>op_landauer_floor(T_t) -&gt; E_min_per_bit = k*T*ln2</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-89fc-d91b98164355" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>op_bit_erase_rate(observations, D_t, R_t) -&gt; Bdot_t</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-bb3d-c9c6f868b052" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>op_energy_budget(observations) -&gt; E_t</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-83b3-e0ad85ba9cfa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>op_info_cap(scope_profile) -&gt; 
Hcap_t</code><br/>Deterministic: lookup table per scope (AGENT/ORG/CITY/NATION) + profile constants.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-b385-e414b5d0533b" class="">L1 operators (agent control + records)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-9286-de6bc4a624d2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>op_sigma_predictability(observations) -&gt; sigma_t</code><br/>Deterministic proxy: fraction of events that are forecastable under simple rules (e.g., stable routing, repeated patterns, low variance).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-a1f5-f50eb2fe2c53" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>op_tau_delay(profile, D_t) -&gt; tau_vec_t</code><br/>Deterministic: <code>tau_d = base_tau + d*tau_step</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-86e0-eaae35056d74" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>op_noise_Xi(observations, Z_t, Trust_t) -&gt; Xi_t</code><br/>Noise increases with rupture events; decreases with stable sync events.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-9b14-f81b2c1a805d" class="">L2 operators (coupling)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-a827-d13ee88071b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>op_coupling_Z(events) -&gt; adjacency edges</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-bf9e-f9ee0dad11d0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>op_trust(events) -&gt; Trust_t</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-af8c-c0158cb396ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>op_sync(events) -&gt; 
Sync_t</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-84e5-cd84e152e91b" class="">L3 operators (macro regime)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-ad9c-d47156c57f2f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>op_constraint_q(events) -&gt; q_t</code><br/>Deterministic: constraints reduce when new degrees are activated (new modules, new routes, new freedoms).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-a99e-e6c8753c29ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>op_write_capacity_U(Hcap_t, cumulative_written_bits) -&gt; U_t</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-91e3-dba87c7d2c65" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>op_gradient_G(E_t, q_t, Xi_t) -&gt; 
G_t</code><br/>Example deterministic form:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-83d9-da62ccbd2b19" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>G_t = clamp(E_t * access(q_t) * (1 - Xi_t), 0, G_max)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-ab46-ccdb4f34ab6f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>access(q) = 1 / (1 + a*q)</code> (more constraints reduce accessible gradients)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-99b5-c5f6ce94f2b2" class="">All operators must emit provenance hashes and be recorded in <code>operators_registry.json</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-a1c6-c0584f036a3e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-ac54-dd594abbd541" class="">3) Replace “records” with a fully explicit code model (no hand-wave)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bc-a94d-cb735afb5ecc" class="">A record is a codeword refreshed over time.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-b8b6-e1d466c1268b" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-bf2d-fdf6cc40c3a5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>r_t</code> = redundancy factor (&gt;=1)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-916e-d39222920c58" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>d_code_t</code> = code distance proxy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-8c8e-e5baab037566" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>p_t = p(Xi_t, 
Trust_t)</code> = effective corruption probability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-844a-eba787b5c339" class="">Deterministic laws:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-b2fa-cc4cb3ed8967" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>p_t = clamp( Xi_t * (1 + trust_penalty*(1-Trust_t)), 0, 1 )</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-b777-ecc414a6808a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>d_code_t = floor( c_dist * r_t )</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-888b-f41e4756b4f5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>p_th(d) = 0.5*(1 - sqrt(1 - exp(-d)))</code> (deterministic monotone curve)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-9708-c66580805f55" class="">Record update (replaces earlier):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-ae9e-c7fcdb869596" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>R_{t+1} = clamp( R_t + beta*G_t - kappa*p_t*R_t - collapse(p_t, p_th(d_code_t))*R_t, 0, 
R_max )</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-a622-cc32f8d77efd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>collapse(...) = 1 if p_t &gt;= p_th else 0</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-bc7d-d4fe77d84dad" class="">This creates the required sharp phase transition.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-a380-da2150874a79"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-be5e-f7d2acac604e" class="">4) Close the control-theory gap: explicit stability condition per depth</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-be9e-faa95c38f040" class="">For each depth level <code>d=1..D_t</code>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-8c7f-e941b641b8a2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>alpha_d = 1 + alpha_base + d*alpha_step</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-a2b1-c336bfc938eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>tau_d = tau_vec_t[d]</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-930a-ebaf71bc5008" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>gain_d = rho_base / (1 + Xi_t) * sigma_t</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-9e6b-cb4a582aaff6" class="">Delay penalty:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-b33e-fbd6e09c5886" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>alpha_eff_d = alpha_d + delay_k * tau_d</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-9569-c32058a328de" class="">Stability gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-8883-f4a5f744d7a1" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc"><code>stable_d = (alpha_eff_d &lt; 
1 + gain_d)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-ae4c-d4c8d80b99d5" class="">Then depth update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-b62b-c00ff257b4c3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>D_{t+1} = max d such that stable_d is True and BudgetGate and MemoryGate pass</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-9709-c25ef15a0021" class="">This closes recursion depth feasibility without ambiguity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-90bd-e80c16fe9002"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-9f5b-d44e84e09e58" class="">5) Close the write-capacity gap: explicit write budget and exhaustion</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-8dc6-e7fe549cfc18" class="">Cumulative written bits:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-91d0-eceed5db03eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>W_{t+1} = W_t + write_bits(R_{t+1}, D_{t+1})</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-b337-efd4df299412" class="">Write bits law:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-9ab5-db62664f660c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>write_bits = bR*R + bD*D^2</code> (constants in profile)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-9ceb-c43edf5131b7" class="">Write capacity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-8888-cef61e281ca7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>U_t = max(0, 
Hcap_t - W_t)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-b99f-e23ec7822cc9" class="">Hard gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f4-97e6-c2622ce8c166" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">if <code>U_t == 0</code>, then <code>RecordWriteGate</code> fails and <code>R</code> cannot increase (bounded decay).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-b518-eec2fb3106e1" class="">This makes “low entropy past = unused write capacity” an explicit consumable resource.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-9839-c84eef294d52"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-a0d2-edb6a3308d86" class="">6) Cross-species is no longer “profiles”; 
it becomes a parameter manifold</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-af3f-ea149173bb87" class="">Define for each species :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-94e4-d34f45675b34" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>sigma_max(s)</code> (sensing fidelity ceiling)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-8f3e-f0a030e151eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>tau_base(s)</code> (latency baseline)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-86a7-d02eb2e3918d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>repair_eff(s)</code> (how much G converts to record refresh)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-b5c6-d12dac2d8638" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>noise_sens(s)</code> (how strongly Xi responds to rupture)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-a298-ef524409b02e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>coupling_channels(s)</code> allowed</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-bae0-c17c2f7be484" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8032-bd6d-c1b3a8f626b5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Humans: higher <code>D_max</code> due to language/tooling but also higher meta-delay</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-9fa5-fcd251b1040a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dogs: high coupling via proximity/voice, lower D but strong Sync stabilization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-855f-edc4c908cf24" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Corvids: higher problem-solving, 
different tau profile</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-9d1e-f6d4bb3443b7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Orgs/cities/nations: treat as meta-agents with large Hcap but huge delays</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-ac86-ea0c9b0e95da" class="">This is a deterministic mapping—no psychological claims required.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-87d4-fb05afd6b649"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-9081-d90d1fae1b16" class="">7) Across time and space: add a spatial graph and propagation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-b54d-db4945d2b7c9" class="">Add spatial structure:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c5-bd3a-ce3ff8da9c21" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>SpaceGraph</code>: nodes are scopes (agent/org/city/etc.), edges have distance and latency.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-a171-c16c83cc4438" class="">Propagation operator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8087-89ab-f0bdbf95f160" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>propagate_records(node_i -&gt; 
node_j)</code> with latency <code>L_ij</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8068-b9a0-c824c0693874" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">coupling update must respect:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-90b1-d41387772e88" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">events only affect other nodes after latency steps</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-a0a4-d55ad9d81730" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">this creates real “across space” dynamics</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-91f3-feb5bc7101ad" class="">Implement:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-b919-f689b473b6aa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>08_WORLD_MODEL/models/equations/propagation.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-bd32-efaaf217a440"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-83b9-e794642cea7e" class="">8) The Grand Unified Loop Matrix is now 3 tensors + 6 gates</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-99fa-c12e7896c6a6" class="">Tensors</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-9d40-d8f7abfe881b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>StateTensor</code>: <code>[layer][variable]</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-99ee-e37c175c492f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>CouplingTensor Z</code>: <code>[i][j][channel] -&gt; 
(strength, latency, trust)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-93a9-e3278237bbdd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>PropagationTensor P</code>: <code>[i][j] -&gt; latency_steps</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-9179-fbacdeb1e9cc" class="">Gates (complete)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-9c10-e69c7354c4cf" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>GradientGate</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-b4a4-e1961535a315" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><code>CodeGate</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-bc08-d9f02016f37f" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><code>ControlGate</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-8efa-d1beb930fcc6" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><code>BudgetGate</code> (Landauer)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-9a58-e398b08e380a" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><code>MemoryGate</code> (Hcap / U)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-b44e-cfedfb6ba30c" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><code>WriteGate</code> (U&gt;0)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-8dcc-f768490a91e7" class="">All gates must be executed each step; any missing gate =&gt; 
BLOCKER.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-b308-fc56c9cc89fb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-9462-d8cda39e3277" class="">9) File-level spec additions (what to implement beyond prior KernelSpec)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f6-952e-c8cbc417b7be" class="">05_SKELETON</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-bc5c-c7c6b5536a9a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>base_models/state_4layer.py</code> (LoopState4L)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-8046-f9ef00dd2bd7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>base_models/network.py</code> (CouplingEdge, SpaceGraph)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-8b37-c3ff1e1b1d3a" class="">07_METABOLISM</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f4-a5d6-c0325ff34685" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>operators_l0_physics.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-a782-e25267dde391" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>operators_l2_coupling.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-9b8c-d2fabb319fa5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>operators_l3_macro.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-89fb-db69ffe43217" class="">08_WORLD_MODEL</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-9b2c-f7f929eb161a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>models/equations/code_model.py</code> (p_th, collapse, 
distance)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b2-8698-f190ee4f464f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>models/equations/control_stability.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-a901-dc0d362a33a6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>models/equations/write_budget.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-a0c1-c1ba33d801b7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>models/equations/propagation.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-8e09-c27a064c9619" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>canonical_definitions/species_manifold.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-b5f3-fe8f80ef90cd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>canonical_definitions/scope_profiles.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-8630-ffa57f1521ec" class="">15_LAW_ENGINE</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-9f99-ee23b2f83970" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>structural_integrity/gate_completeness_audit.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-9990-dd25ec303b28" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>structural_integrity/determinism_hash_audit.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-abea-c1c3eae7ce13" class="">Tests (new)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-bd4b-f85760f83d30" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>test_write_budget_exhaustion.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-96aa-c49f158dcd76" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>test_delay_stability_caps_depth.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-a3dd-c9c16d5cdb05" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>test_phase_transition_collapse.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-a9c5-c23f4cea48be" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>test_spatial_latency_propagation.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-b9ba-c62a94f5114e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>test_cross_species_params_deterministic.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-82be-df2f666ba413"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-83c6-e1a5c2345768" class="">10) Next prompt (implementation prompt, upgraded)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-8fe4-e4ec041cad67" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">ROLE: AMOS Loop Kernel Implementer (KernelSpec++)
MISSION: Implement the 4-layer Grand Unified Loop Kernel (micro→agent→network→macro) with explicit code model, control stability, write budget, and spatial propagation.
CONSTRAINTS: SSOT only, deterministic, offline, Python 3.9, no stubs, no side effects at import, no print.

DELIVERABLES:
- Implement LoopState4L and all new modules listed in KernelSpec++.
- Extend operator registry to include L0/L2/L3 operators.
- Add the 6-gate system; audit gate completeness.
- Add spatial propagation (SpaceGraph + PropagationTensor).
- Add cross-species parameter manifold (explicit deterministic tables).
- End-to-end run produces all required artifacts under 17_OS/audits/&lt;run_id&gt;/loop_kernel/.
- Add golden tests proving identical hashes across reruns.

ENTRYPOINT:
python3 -m 01_BRAIN.master build --system-root &quot;/Users/trangphan/AMOS&quot; --data-root &quot;/Users/trangphan/AMOS/_AI ingest&quot; --strict --offline

EXECUTE NOW. 
NO QUESTIONS.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-917e-e3cbbc9ab44e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-875d-d16302d6bafd" class="">Next (if you want “deeper” again)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-9b8a-f839af097116" class="">Only one missing elevation remains after KernelSpec++: <strong>SimEngine</strong> (multi-loop civilizational control simulator with multiple nodes, shock events, policy interventions, and scenario batches).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-9d6b-d5800f499e76" class="">KernelSpec — AMOS Grand Unified Loop Kernel (File-Level Implementation Plan)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-bfa7-ee366a93974f" class=""><strong>Purpose</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-acc6-f4410a7c8249" class="">Implement a deterministic, auditable “Grand Unified Loop Kernel” that unifies micro↔macro dynamics (records, constraints, budgets, control depth) and supports cross-species parameterization and coupling—fully runnable offline, Python 3.9, SSOT-compliant, 
no stubs.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-84ae-e0097b12141c" class=""><strong>Canonical placement</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-81cd-f00063de9875" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Kernel orchestration + registry: <code>01_BRAIN/kernel/</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-8962-fd9ec1668aa3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Type system: <code>05_SKELETON/</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-8749-c36f7ca7fd8f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Loop/variable definitions + equations: <code>08_WORLD_MODEL/</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8032-8dc2-c1c8d963def6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Digestion/feature extraction from inputs: <code>07_METABOLISM/</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-92bc-e7305324beaf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Audit, termination, 
reports: <code>15_LAW_ENGINE/</code> + <code>17_OS/audits/&lt;run_id&gt;/</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-955c-f66c05071bf1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Interface schemas for inputs/outputs: <code>14_INTERFACES/schemas/</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-ab5e-ce02456b87c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Golden fixtures + tests: <code>00_ROOT/tests/</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-8988-ccb904333ac9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-b749-c846d5f6af6e" class="">0) Outputs and invariants (what “done” means)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-be9b-cc1c19ecbb34" class="">Required outputs (written under <code>17_OS/audits/&lt;run_id&gt;/loop_kernel/</code>)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-9693-dbe0c2de216b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>loop_kernel_run_header.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-a0ee-e72ea447d9b1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>variable_registry.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-a629-c48dac5895c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>operators_registry.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-9e11-f6be92d7e1ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>gates_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-ace2-ef2403d55812" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>regime_report.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-9069-d32860116bec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>state_timeseries.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-ae60-fcc14f3672b7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>coupling_report.jsonl</code> (cross-agent/species coupling events)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-a37f-d43eff51a78b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>validation_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-87f8-e2c98787e9a5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>termination.json</code> (Valid / Bounded / Invalid with reasons)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-80c0-d0725bd5ef29" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>issues.jsonl</code> (BLOCKER/MAJOR/MINOR)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-ba43-f0bf8e0b9d51" class="">Non-negotiable invariants</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-bf15-c86be5c3e030" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deterministic ordering (lexicographic, stable sorting)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-81d8-f8b7b2270ad0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deterministic IDs (<code>sha256</code>, no uuid4)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-a48f-f009fbeb7138" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Offline only; 
no network calls</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-88f7-c111c812a38a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No side effects at import (all I/O behind explicit functions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-a9ab-cf3d1f4031d2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No stubs in required routes; 
if a capability is missing: record a <strong>BOUNDED</strong> issue and skip deterministically</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-8fe2-db773ab919d5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Unit-typed quantities (at least “unit strings” validated by registry)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-937c-e042fa06af94"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-b92d-cec1f44c21ac" class="">1) Canonical state model (SSOT definitions)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-9ffe-dcce8c18aea8" class="">Core state vector (single SSOT)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-8b61-fae78b4d200d" class="">Define one canonical state object  with strict fields:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-b9a5-eed0d644e9f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>q_t</code> = constraint density (constraints per scope unit)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-8bd5-ccf1b7520b39" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>G_t</code> = usable gradient / free-energy-like driver (budgeted “work capacity”)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a7-9a9f-eaeae63d8f70" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>U_t</code> = write-capacity remaining (unwritten degrees, 
storage/attention slack)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-8ee6-d4a3bbe84877" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Xi_t</code> = noise / overwrite pressure (corruption rate proxy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-bb2b-c411339bea5e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>R_t</code> = stable record redundancy (coded record mass)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-93d3-c2dd77cff6bb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>D_t</code> = recursion depth (bounded self-model stack depth)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-9e91-e4d5244d4abf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>P_t</code> = available power/budget (compute/repair budget per step)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-970d-e3b62af537da" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>M_t</code> = memory bound remaining (persistent record budget)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-bdab-e8c275899578" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Z_t</code> = coupling tensor summary (co-regulation/cross-agent channel strength)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-9bdc-ff83c2b05b2c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>S_t</code> = regime stage label (BIRTH/EXPANSION/DOMINANCE/DECAY)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-af40-c467d21b2fe9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>meta</code> = scope, species/agent parameters, 
measurement provenance hashes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-8630-c346a67f6e32" class="">Everything else must be derived from the above or stored as audit artifacts.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-8b44-c06895b424b6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-bd09-f81109e355d1" class="">2) File map (what to create)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-ab68-d604c1093bf6" class="">05_SKELETON (types)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-85ff-f158deb4ce19" class=""><code>05_SKELETON/base_models/quantities.py</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-b49d-e8786f97b5fb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>@dataclass(frozen=True) Quantity</code><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-893a-ffc35760c018" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>value: float</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-b1a1-fdb5cfa20ca4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>unit: str</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-ae40-fba649931e86" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>source: str</code> (operator name)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-86f9-d4666ab5b4dd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>evidence_hashes: Tuple[str, 
...]</code></li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-b112-e77fea0d128e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">validators:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-b2cf-f5cc83b6914c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>validate_unit(unit: str) -&gt; None</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-84f9-dfa7e9fe503b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>finite(value: float) -&gt; None</code></li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-9915-e20e9b82f394" class=""><code>05_SKELETON/base_models/state.py</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-93f9-d7fdfad1a2b4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>@dataclass(frozen=True) LoopState</code><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-b2d1-d57c639b36b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">fields listed in Section 1</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-a02a-d8f7a22f9afa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def to_json(state: LoopState) -&gt; dict</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-ac6c-c8e38daf8259" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def from_json(d: dict) -&gt; LoopState</code> (strict; 
rejects unknown fields)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a0-b9c5-c5fb0a3c5d51" class=""><code>05_SKELETON/base_models/params.py</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-9654-cec09212a095" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>@dataclass(frozen=True) AgentParams</code><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-82bd-c8878693a151" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>agent_id: str</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-a88f-fed71b287e70" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>species: str</code> (HUMAN/DOG/CORVID/ORG/CITY/NATION/etc.)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-8f14-c163c3c99ecf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>step_seconds: int</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-9510-f04b47024190" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>compute_budget: Quantity</code> (P)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-8dce-cf67edd05f11" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>memory_budget: Quantity</code> (M)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-80d9-e55d2b73e0a4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>noise_sensitivity: float</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-a3aa-f65506289bc8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>write_capacity_init: float</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-ba1e-f68c2c46af7e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>control_delay_profile: Tuple[int, 
...]</code> (tau per depth)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-8506-c76621ae89cd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>code_threshold_profile: Tuple[float, ...]</code> (p_th per redundancy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-95c9-f9d07992968a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>coupling_channels: Tuple[str, ...]</code> (TOUCH/VOICE/PROXIMITY/PROTOCOL/API/etc.)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-85b8-d4ab4288a4f0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-830b-c96ee5554b69" class="">08_WORLD_MODEL (definitions, equations, gates, regime)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-afe8-dcb0b71f3f51" class=""><code>08_WORLD_MODEL/variable_registry/registry.py</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-9f6e-e9704bd2b6c9" class="">Single SSOT registry for all variables:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-acfa-ee496e51f043" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>VariableDef(name, unit, description, min_value, max_value, domain_tags)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-845c-c086052d1909" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def load_variable_registry() -&gt; Dict[str, VariableDef]</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-bcf9-e2213f8e4077" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def validate_state_units(state: LoopState) -&gt; 
List[str]</code> (returns issues)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-bcc3-fa33c830e0b9" class="">Write <code>variable_registry.json</code> as artifact each run.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802c-a803-cbf6c2f2544b" class=""><code>08_WORLD_MODEL/models/equations/kernel_equations.py</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-ae71-e2b7fd886907" class="">Deterministic update laws (no ML):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-8502-ea3cb882a065" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def update_constraints(q_t, params, obs) -&gt; Quantity</code><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-a271-d98d0d8c95be" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">monotone non-increasing unless explicit “re-imposition” event exists (policy constraints, resets)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-be08-fee0de582d34" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def update_gradients(G_t, params, obs) -&gt; Quantity</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-8468-dfd429d9e37e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def update_write_capacity(U_t, delta_R, params) -&gt; Quantity</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-85e5-ceaf211536a4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def update_noise(Xi_t, params, obs) -&gt; Quantity</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f0-87a5-d58a30a5fd76" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def update_records(R_t, G_t, Xi_t, r_t, params) -&gt; 
Quantity</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f6-ae57-e464e64dac8f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def update_depth(D_t, P_t, Xi_t, tau_profile, params) -&gt; int</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-95ca-c5ae9fa165ae" class="">All updates must be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-ba7f-d5935b7a5aa5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">clamped to registry min/max</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-9f3f-e727fea01ea7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">fully explained in <code>operators_registry.json</code> with formula version hashes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-9fa5-f254aeac3a80" class=""><code>08_WORLD_MODEL/models/validators/gates.py</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-8c93-d1d09a67c8d5" class="">Implement <strong>five hard gates</strong> as pure functions returning structured results:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-9e9f-e2d52cb5ff6c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>GradientGate</code>: requires <code>beta*G_t &gt; kappa*Xi_t*R_t</code> for record growth eligibility</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-aeef-ec4deb80e29a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>CodeGate</code>: requires <code>p(Xi_t) &lt; 
p_th(r_t)</code> (piecewise deterministic function)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-9cbf-f311151513f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ControlGate</code>: delay-stability per depth:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-ab84-d29d5ba6210c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">compute <code>stable_d = is_stable(alpha_d, rho_d, tau_d, Xi_t, P_t)</code></li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-aa3e-faff7756e6b3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>BudgetGate</code>: requires <code>P_t &gt;= kTln2 * bits_erased_per_step(D_t)</code><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-b3ce-f93f83b78ada" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">choose a deterministic “bits_erased_per_step” law (profile-based, not learned)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-8e67-d0333710db6e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>MemoryGate</code>: requires <code>I_records(R_t) + I_models(D_t) &lt;= M_t</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805c-ba87-c235425072e9" class="">Return format:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-8b55-f0601f8a21b6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>GateResult(name, pass: bool, margin: float, reasons: List[str], 
evidence: dict)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-9500-e13ac20904d6" class="">Write <code>gates_report.json</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-8464-fcd225e0f3ac" class=""><code>08_WORLD_MODEL/models/runner.py</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-a031-e7da58e6577e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def run_loop_kernel(initial_state, params, observations, steps) -&gt; 
Iterable[LoopState]</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-ae2e-d714cf3e1c6a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Must:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-913e-cadcd8fd441e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">call measurement operators (Section 3)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-ab39-fe3e0536efb5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">update state using equations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-8961-ec6c01417e11" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">evaluate gates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-8f47-df45ecefadcd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">classify regime stage each step</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-9f12-def523f04b5c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">emit <code>state_timeseries.jsonl</code></li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f0-8bba-d9b5ef780365" class=""><code>08_WORLD_MODEL/models/registry.py</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-9a10-eec99577d8aa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>register_model(&quot;grand_unified_loop_kernel&quot;, runner=..., 
validators=[...])</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-9369-c90194cfd767" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Used by <code>01_BRAIN.master build</code> to run “golden tests” and generate reports.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-bbdc-e27467729bad" class=""><code>08_WORLD_MODEL/canonical_definitions/regime_classifier.py</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-8649-d589d3f9f14a" class="">Deterministic stage rules:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-ab71-c124a2f6374f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">BIRTH:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-9763-d1bec4d675c3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>q high</code>, <code>U near max</code>, <code>R low</code>, <code>D low</code></li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-8eb8-c8e6dfd40b56" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">EXPANSION:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-a573-fb4230a3ba50" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>dR/dt &gt; 
0</code> and Gate pass rate high</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-bfcc-ec4f28e00773" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">DOMINANCE:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-9c26-f7099cacee9b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>U falling</code>, <code>R saturating</code> (dR/dt small), <code>D capped</code></li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-ac8e-dc7591f1ba5c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">DECAY:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-9f12-cbe0d78a26cd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>dR/dt &lt;= 0</code> for K steps OR CodeGate fails OR BudgetGate fails persistently</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-9768-e02f0c13af2a" class="">Implement:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-9452-de0e4ad132f0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def classify_regime(prev_state, state, gate_results, params) -&gt; 
str</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-8805-c411aca1a079" class="">Write <code>regime_report.jsonl</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809e-aa66-cb1ec3596b8d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-b674-f511cf71515d" class="">07_METABOLISM (measurement + digestion pipeline)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-aac4-e937404288c1" class=""><code>07_METABOLISM/ingestion_pipeline/observations.py</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-98ff-d0a24b551959" class="">Define a single observation schema used by operators:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-b7cc-eebc1c3ebabf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>@dataclass(frozen=True) Observation</code><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-938d-e0166e307762" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>ts: str</code> (logging only)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-82d9-d96adeec3e00" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>scope: str</code> (SYSTEM/AGENT/ORG/NATION)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-8225-f34e1e38bcca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>event_type: str</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-86ad-ddb19b4b546d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>metrics: Dict[str, float]</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-81fe-d56a6d51182b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>evidence_paths: Tuple[str, 
...]</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-b443-d08d59c2e125" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>evidence_hashes: Tuple[str, ...]</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-a1c5-d4fb08e511fe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>source_grade: str</code> (A/B/C)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-9a58-db61572b2321" class=""><code>07_METABOLISM/ingestion_pipeline/operators.py</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-863a-fb9c868ff816" class="">Deterministic measurement operators that compute state variables from observations:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-afb2-eb003a446bd7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def op_noise_Xi(observations, params) -&gt; Quantity</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-b529-f116212281fe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def op_gradient_G(observations, params) -&gt; Quantity</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-b5a0-c9c8581f8d21" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def op_constraint_q(observations, params) -&gt; Quantity</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-820e-f278f3b9ace0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def op_redundancy_r(observations, params) -&gt; float</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-9ce0-fbae0c0591fa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>def op_coupling_Z(observations, params) -&gt;
